- **da-ingest-case**: 录入分析案例到知识库。从分析报告中提取可复用的分析框架和指标定义，转为标准模板。支持扫描 inbox/cases/ 或直接粘贴内容。 ([guoqiaoZhou/data-analysis-plugin](https://github.com/guoqiaoZhou/data-analysis-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/da-ingest-case) · ★1)

- **da-jia-answer**: Ask one question to multiple configured LLMs simultaneously. Auto-discovers models on the user's system, lets them pick which to query, then calls them in parallel and presents answers side-by-side for comparison. Zero manual configuration — just select models and ask. Triggers: everyone answers, multi-model compare, ask all models, A/B/C test, side-by-side LLM, "da-jia-answer". 一个问题同时问多个已配置的大模型，并行发问、多栏对比呈现各模型回答。主 Agent 自动探测用户系统上已配置的大模型，列出让用户勾选，然后并行调用、多栏对比。全程用户只需勾选模型，无需手动配置。 触发词：大家都来回答、三路对比、多个 AI 都回答、对比回答、大家怎么说、你们几个怎么看 ([totwo2/da-jia-answer](https://github.com/totwo2/da-jia-answer/tree/HEAD/skills/da-jia-answer))

- **dai-dai-dai**: Piano di giornata e standup - trasforma il caos in cosa si fa oggi o questa settimana, con al massimo tre priorità, le cose in ritardo, i blocchi con l'azione per sbloccarli e chi serve e per cosa. Funziona per il PM da solo ("cosa faccio oggi?") e per la squadra (standup giornaliero, messaggio del lunedì, check-in settimanale). Usala quando l'utente dice "dai dai dai", "cosa faccio oggi?", "pianifichiamo la settimana", "prepara lo standup", "scrivi il messaggio del lunedì per la squadra", "sono bloccato", "da dove riparto?", "ho mille cose da fare oggi". Non usarla per aggiornare capi, clien… ([alelu-interest/boris-school](https://github.com/alelu-interest/boris-school/tree/HEAD/plugins/boris-pm/skills/dai-dai-dai))

- **DAi-paper**: Recent-paper reader for non-academics. Based on an optimized adaptation of Li Jigang's skill style (https://github.com/lijigang/ljg-skills). Primarily reads AIGC, spatial intelligence, and interaction papers from the last 1-2 years, then explains them through this structure: source judgment, background and problem, theory foundation, translated core concepts, methods and results, and review insights. When syncing to Notion, it should default to creating or updating a database entry, not a standalone page. ([DAi0519/Skilllll](https://github.com/DAi0519/Skilllll/tree/HEAD/DAi-paper) · ★5)
- **dai-practica-agentes**: Automatiza prácticas de Desarrollo de Agentes Inteligentes (DAI - Clave ACD-2504). Cuenta con la bibliografía y temario oficial del TecNM ITCM precargados. Genera dashboards educativos en Tkinter con diseño Apple Dark, texto bilingüe ES/EN en lenguaje técnico-simple para cualquier ser humano, simulaciones animadas renderizadas en 2D/2.5D pseudo-3D, carpeta de evidencias con marca Skill Valadez y prompts en PDF de ingeniería inversa. - **dai-yusen-perspective**: 【升级版】戴雨森的深度思维框架与表达方式。基于30+个播客访谈、深度演讲、投资案例分析的全面调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、10条决策启发式和完整的VC思维DNA。 用途：作为AI时代天使投资思维顾问，深度理解技术趋势判断、投资时机选择、创业者评估。 当用户提到「用戴雨森的视角」「雨森会怎么看」「真格视角」「戴雨森模式」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用真格合伙人的角度想想」「如果戴雨森会怎么做」「分析这个AI项目」也应触发。 ([Jadeee04/Yusen-Dai.skill](https://github.com/Jadeee04/Yusen-Dai.skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
